| 정상·장애 process | 기존 candidate body 후 fresh restore-forward→rollback→새 candidate의 restore-forward, 게시 후 forward/replay, ACK-loss·원 요청 재시도·재시작을 각 본문으로 실행 | 첫 요청 Applied, 의도적 같은 요청 retry Reconciled·추가 게시0, actual selection·Catalog/Source/ACK·query gold·loss/duplicate 결과와 nonzero body |
| 정식 P11 | 기존 `p11-rollback`의 action·독립 observer·승인 Linux target 입력을 원 P11 오너가 결속 | 현재 staged 등록을 해소한 해당 배포/운영 수용. 이 입력은 로컬 코드/owning 수정을 막는 조건으로 사용하지 않음 |

현재 등록된 candidate와 정상 요청은 S에서 각각 `tools/testing/run-suite-v1.sh native-candidate-stale-then-published-legacy-process-owner --resolve-only`, `tools/testing/run-suite-v1.sh native-installed-forward-rollback-forward-process-owner --resolve-only`로 확인할 수 있다. 두 selector는 각각 expected body 1개로 등록돼 있다. resolve는 실행 계획만 확인하며 body 성공을 의미하지 않는다. 별도 게시 후/장애 확장이 필요하면 같은 원 registry 오너가 정확한 본문과 함께 등록한다. 이전 실행 영수증의 요청 ID를 현재 source의 body 성공으로 재사용하지 않는다.

이전 패킷 갱신에서 기존 `--resolve-only` 명령은 exit0으로 exact selector, `quanta-cli-server.no-default.61b2510e7d2a`와 expected body 1개를 반환했다. 그 명령의 테스트 실행 수는 0이다. 최종 문서 감사는 아래 확인 결과에 별도로 기록한다.

**이전 문서 경로·형식 감사는 VERIFIED다.** 실제 ordinary V9 직접 terminal·deferred replay, Source→V9 ingress, FD6 fact, Java FULL dispatch·feature 전달, resident restart와 selection/journal cycle을 원 소스에 대조했다. NC-D·NC-F·README·DISPATCH·Tantivy 전환·운영 runbook의 복구 지시를 같은 현재 목록으로 정리했다. 기존 doc-path lint의 `link_issue`로 여섯 문서의 165개 링크를 검사해 오류 0개였고 whitespace·현재 anchor·code fence 검사도 통과했다. 변경 범위는 관련 작업·운영 문서다. 제품 Rust·설치 본문은 **NOT_RUN**이며 각 원 owning 큐의 현재 cohort 검증을 따른다.

**이전 적대·객관 감사는 당시 소스의 정적 범위에서 VERIFIED다.** 초기 Java 생산자의 `OriginalJava`/snapshot None과 실제 child writer의 `Java`/snapshot·handoff 요구, 후보 FullGraph의 Java wire None과 Python-only DFG ingress의 Some(true) 요구를 직접 대조했다. 두 입력 규약 보완을 별도 필수 패킷으로 드러냈다. synthetic initializer는 explicit constructor와 provenance가 다르고 이 fixture의 도달이 입증되지 않아 정상 후보 gate에서 분리했다. 관련 일곱 작업·운영 문서를 갱신하고 DOD를 포함한 여덟 문서의 local link/anchor 249개·공통 목록 참조·여덟 작업 행·whitespace·code fence/details를 검사해 오류0이었다. 실제 Rust 재현과 signed process 본문은 **NOT_RUN**이다.

현재 코드 종료 범위는 위 D1–D3다. 이미 연결된 cold graph/DFG·초기 ACK/signed/FD6/Q·정상 fixture는 현재 owning 검증에서 해당 원인 결함이 확인될 때만 보완한다. fresh generation/Store·mixed Attach·synthetic initializer를 cold 후보의 새 선행 작업으로 추가하지 않는다. signed Linux process 수용과 정식 P11은 각 범위에서 따로 판정하며 결과 전까지 전체 `source_ready=false`를 유지한다.

**직전 병렬 배정 문서 검증은 VERIFIED다.** `tools/ci/lint/lint-doc-paths.py`의 `link_issue`를 사용한 관련 7문서·248링크 검사, 다섯 코드 레인·네 기존 채팅 ID·고유 anchor·whitespace·fence/details 대조와 scoped `git diff --check`를 통과했다. 독립 검토에 따라 F wire codec과 Source join의 range/sort/charge 작성권을 분리했다. 해당 계획 턴에서는 production 코드 변경·새 Rust/설치 본문·타 채팅 발송을 수행하지 않았다. 후속 D 코드 보완은 상단 구현 절을 따른다.

## NC-D-HARDEN-03 · 전체 자기 변경 검토·수리

### Scope / attribution / intent-to-proof

**아래는 F78/F81 고정 입력의 과거 실행·귀속 기록이다.** 현행 publisher/codec/tests·launcher/test 파일은 이 절의 SHA와 달라 과거14/125를 현재 통과로 재사용하지 않는다. 현재 작성 상태와 source별 수용은 [최신 session-hardening](#d-session-hardening-current-20261116)을 따른다. 당시 receiver 미구현 기록을 현재 신규 구현 배정으로 읽거나 원 정상 복구 DoD를 삭제하는 데 사용하지 않는다.

- 현재 사용자 `session-hardening` 및 작업한 것 전체 보완 요청에 따른 calling-thread 범위다. Q HEAD `ade5fadc1a084fbb81eba831861e996081d80867`, 공유 main 기존 dirty/원 writer/별도 worktree를 보존했다. Rust publisher 네 파일은 D introduced, public module/공유 Q callback·CLI·Cargo는 F owner, Semantica Host/SDK/Original store는 기존 공급자 owner다. Python diff에는 인수 전 F handoff가 포함돼 전체 dirty를 D 도입으로 귀속하지 않는다. runbook 전체 문서 정리는 다른 writer 것이며 D recovery hunk만 대조했다.

| --- | --- | --- |
| Legacy→Native FULL handoff | pre-existing F producer tuple → D가 강화한 switch/main → 원 verifier/CAS | tuple 전체 필수화·기존 CLI/CAS 회귀는 같은 source의125 범위에서 satisfied. 실제 설치 producer parity는 missing이며 fixture tuple로 대체하지 않는다. |

| Canonical Rust file publisher/codec | D introduced → Unix public module 및 실제 파일 transaction | 아래2개 introduced 행동 결함 repaired/VERIFIED14. 현재 production installed receiver는 missing; generic callback을 Source permission으로 승격하지 않는다. |
| Rust 실제 FS/golden/negative 회귀 | D introduced → actual NativeResourceLedger/flock/files/WorkBudget | old9 PASS+새2 실제 RED 이후 repair, 원11 오라클 byte 보존+추가3 hostile cases 실제14 PASS. Source/Q 권한 mock으로 정상 rollback을 주장하지 않는다. |

### Confirmed findings / canonical repairs

1. **HARDEN-03-A / introduced / persistence failure phase.** 이전 `with_locked_deployment_selection_v1`은 실제 publish+directory fsync 뒤 callback이 `RefusedBeforeRename`를 반환해도 그대로 전파했다. 실제 selection이 바뀌었는데 "not changed"로 보고해 재시도 판단을 틀리게 한다. 원 publisher 객체에 BeforeRename/RenameAttempted/Durable을 기록하고 rename syscall **전**부터 이후 오류·재시도까지 유지한다. callback 후 오류를 실제 phase로 정규화하며, 불확실한 publish를 삼킨 성공/같은 객체의 재시도로 초기화하지 않는다. 원 request cancellation/deadline도 scope 반환 전에 확인한다. Source/ACK 승인이나 새 issuer를 추가하지 않았다.
2. **HARDEN-03-B / introduced / repeated full binary reads.** `read_document`마다 같은 binary를 current/candidate별로 해싱해 acquire/pre-temp/post-temp 세 시점에서 총6번 읽었다. 고정512KiB 동일 binary·3MiB caller budget이 실제 ResourceLimited로 실패했다. codec의 wire decode와 binary authentication을 분리하고 **동일 시점의 exact(path,digest) pair만** 한 번 검증한다. 서로 다른 binary는 각각 검사하고 세 검증 시점 모두 보존한다. mtime/inode cache·phase 간 digest reuse·cap/budget 증가가 없다. 같은 binding에서6→3 full hash이며 시스템 성능/SDK60/제품 수용 승리가 아니다.
3. **HARDEN-03-C / introduced / compilation.** 첫 actual owning compiler에서 read_to_end usize의 미사용 결과가 `-D unused-results`에 걸렸다. 반환 byte count를 `_read_bytes`로 바인딩했다. 이 compile/body0는 행동 RED로 계산하지 않았다.

### Executed proof / source binding

- F78 first attempt: `/tmp/qidx-f78-deployment-red-70c7wy3y/owning.stdout`, exit101, **body0**. D가 실제 error 위치를 확인하고 C만 먼저 수리했다.
- F78 actual RED: `/tmp/qidx-f78-deployment-body-zfdwi48c/owning.stdout`, **11 executed / 9 PASS / 2 FAIL / exit100**,0.569s. `callback_failure_after_durable_publish_is_never_before_rename`는 먼저 실제 target bytes를 확인한 뒤 "changed selection reported no mutation"으로 실패했다. `same_binary_three_phase_verification_fits_fixed_work_budget`는 실제512KiB file과 고정3MiB에서 ResourceLimited로 실패했다. old9는 모두 통과했다. Source repair 전 실제 behavioral RED2다.
- F81 actual GREEN: `/tmp/qidx-f81-runtime-kernel-_mr0_ew4/owning.stdout`, **21 executed / 21 PASS / 1633 skipped / exit0**,20.975s. D가 raw stdout에서 **14개 고유 deployment_selection::tests PASS**를 직접 추출하고 command.json/sources.json을 읽었다. 나머지 Kernel4/Text cold1/daemon2는 별도 scope이며 설치 rollback/restore-forward가 아니다.

### Residual / state

기계적 publisher 수리의 과거 판정은 **VERIFIED14@F81**이다. 현재 SHA의 Rust 본문 수용은 별도이며, 원 의도의 전체 Source→fence→두 저장소→실제 설치 helper→launcher 정상 rollback/restore-forward도 여전히 미수용이다. 당시에는 실제 transferred Host+SDK exclusive custody, independent signed pending/outbox/delivery/ACK/floor, 원 FULL baseline과 published Source의 결속, 동일 pre-lock Original budget→Q root authority→fsync를 유지하는 receiver가 필요했다. 후속 작성된 receiver는 위 최신 패킷에서 현재 실행 수용과 구분한다. in-process SQLite native-link 충돌은 Q helper 경계로 조정했고 신규 글로벌 SQLite fork/가짜 Source JSON/boolean/consumer SQL로 우회하지 않는다. F가 generic API visibility와 private actual helper entry integration을 소유한다. local14/Source-ready/static/dispatch를 domain permission·installed acceptance로 올리지 않는다. 전체 NC-D state=partial/source_ready=false. ## Fresh Native candidate preparation · 2026-11-14

| Owner | Implementation | Completion condition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q launcher and BOOT | The launcher stages the exact FULL/AST handoff, drains and reaps its Q child, then publishes the matching clean-drain record before BOOT. | S Legacy recovery and FULL | The candidate retains the committed FULL Source and Original and validates snapshot, generation and execution identity. InputStore restores from committed Source. Before seed publication, the Java pair reads only the manifest-named V7 child under that same FULL and prepares the existing paid HIR and semantic providers. | S Analysis Head import inventory | Candidate selects the exact durable Head, checks its inventory reference against the manifest closure and the genesis root binding where present, and holds the scope guard through FULL visibility. The paid read projects the nested CBOR import inventory and checks its digest and Source/world/extraction. A new target preflights it. The owner regression covers a foreign reference, the exact reference and last-drop refund. |
| S candidate command | The one-shot S producer carries the live Q process lease and selected profile from the launcher profile step across SDK bootstrap. The cold oracle changes both live language files and requires the committed contents in Q. |

**Fresh mixed Original Product genesis — separate initial-generation scope.** The existing Java and Python producers can prepare separate receipt partitions under an authenticated Java+Python Source. The join checks the full Source identity and total membership, compares Python receipt paths with the checked DFG bank, and moves the owners only after Source accepts. The executor feeds the Python projection into the same paid QRA and Native producer used by standalone Python. The V7 reader checks exact coverage of Java HIR and Python supplemental inventory when a portable HIR capsule is present. This does not reopen the already committed mixed FULL candidate restore as missing implementation.

Mixed frontend preparation derives Python normalized facts, catalog correspondence, dispositions and body rows from the exact QRA and retained Parser provider. The mixed member carries its funded path and Parser module identity. Build-context digest input is streamed in the full pin's canonical path order using the existing digest domain. The paid callable collector joins Java's checked retained callable keys with Python QRA declaration keys against the same Original operation, emits stable declaration order and refuses a duplicate. The V7 encoder can stream the full callable path table, both receipt partitions and Java portable HIR from the retained Java inventory. The single and batched cold readers check the two inventory path sets against the full Source pin before reading HIR.

The mixed producer now retains a typed Python import-provider inventory with each normalized member. It uses certified provider surfaces, the existing fingerprint and witness grammar, and the canonical batch report-context digest. The V7 supplemental writer borrows all seven inventory fields from that owner. Legacy Attach still reaches the singleton Original generator through `prepare_complete_generation_v1`. Its mixed preparation owner has no Attach caller. The Java inner artifact encoder still requires snapshot hierarchy, override and callsite sections. Supply those exact sections from the retained Java Parser cohort, then join mixed semantic/Native Product ownership through the existing CAS/manifest commit and locked HEAD readback.

The existing Original Java artifact writer now compares each retained member FileId with both snapshot semantic-file and hierarchy-bundle FileIds inside its existing paid, FileId-ordered visit. Both comparisons use the canonical paid byte-key comparison helper. Count and identity mismatch refuse the artifact; valid wire bytes and digest domains are unchanged. No extra full-cohort traversal was added. This closes a snapshot/census correspondence gap, but does not supply the retained-only hierarchy/dispatch producer or connect mixed Attach. Parser compilation and this behavior are **NOT_RUN** on the new source.

The candidate-only provider preserves the installed provider when one is present. Otherwise it validates the committed FULL and uses the exact BuildContext and canonical Parser on its Python subset. It keeps the Source-backed allocation live through first FULL. Preflight runs before target reservation and only on V9 absence for an already reserved target. The local provider refuses declared dependencies and configured or degraded Python analysis authority until the external-module catalog is bound to that exact build and Source.

**Cold Original module graph — wiring implemented, behavior NOT_RUN.** This supersedes the earlier missing-caller P1. The candidate now derives its Source-bound Original graph before RepoState publication through `prepare_preseed_pair_v7` and `derive_retained_original_import_graph_v11`. It reuses committed FULL text, the canonical Python parser/Java sealed inventory and the existing graph owner; it does not treat V7 import rows alone as resolved Java graph authority. FULL/pin/store/digest/census and Source/Output completion checks remain in the production path. Reuse the current code-repair checkpoint above for regression and runtime acceptance; do not assign another graph builder or caller. Changed and RecordOnly graph paths retain their separate physical publication receipts.

**Java cold HIR repair · 2026-11-15.** The old reader hydrated a Source-only FULL, then called the semantic lender, which necessarily refused with `semantic_artifact_not_hydrated`. The lower FULL loan now reads only its manifest-named Java child with the existing paid CAS reader, raw digest check and fixed V7 envelope decoder. Both single and batch readers retain read/decode DATA through Source completion and preserve the typed Java child cause. The candidate batch borrows the already authenticated FULL instead of rehydrating Source. It validates the exact Java/Python inventory partition and the Java HIR row count before decoding any HIR. No persisted schema or digest domain changes. The owning regression deletes an already hydrated Source CAS member, requires artifact access to succeed from the retained FULL, and then rejects a corrupt or missing Java child before its consumer callback. Static checks do not establish its runtime result.

**Java FULL DFG ingress · 2026-11-15 — wiring implemented, behavior NOT_RUN.** This supersedes the Python-only visitor P1. The current visitor delegates all 14 Java variants to existing actual/typed-empty callsite authority, and the existing ingress consumes the paid reader while retaining its `Some(true)` authority check. The actual Product path supplies the Original completed family and reuses the existing producer, bank and Args. Reader regressions and the strengthened Original Product virtual-call test are authored, not executed proof. No new Java callsite producer/bank or CommonTruth engine is required. The separate paid synthetic callable/index/callee limitation is conditional on the actual accepted Source; the current process fixture has no static/instance initializer. Java-only and requested-frontier expansion remain outside this combined candidate acceptance.

The existing optional-line Original staging and its request-root regression are retained; only the regression currently calls the no-line branch. They do not establish a live synthetic initializer producer. General synthetic authority and callee support are not added to the current candidate work list unless the accepted committed Source requires them. Keep the typed refusal and callee/item join. The regression remains **NOT_RUN** behind the recorded owning compiler failure.

The obsolete preprojection refusal now passes checked Original or carried ImportGraph output to the existing native shard, edge, and RepoMap consumers. Native publication still requires Original graph backing. Optional query-serving enrichment retains its existing failure behavior. Java V7 CAS readback reuses the verified object bytes and removes a redundant envelope decode. The typed semantic readback remains in place.

The preparation step and the resident cutover each own their Original. The durable operation/snapshot and candidate digest bind the steps, and resident recovery independently validates the current committed Source and physical Q candidate before CAS. `NativeOfflineCutoverRequestV1`, its operation ID and journal, FD3–FD6, and schema 2 keep their current format. Q adds Rust command enum variants, so exhaustive downstream matches require an update. Current Native → fresh Native remains gated by the old-root replay horizon.

The launcher supplies the Q image, config-derived socket, root and child PID. `--producer-env` must select the same durable SDK snapshot store as the Legacy service. ## Legacy RepoMap recovery and producer readback · 2026-11-14

- Q verifies a large active RepoMap object using the existing admitted reader, canonical envelope codec and compiled-payload decoder. The private file reader retains the input lease. The decoder charges its own work and temporary allocation and visits each graph or projection row without retaining the tables. The same payload checks run on the serving and recovery paths. The compiler now checks the aggregate projected preview bytes and symbol count against the same values it records in the resource receipt. This fixes a debug assertion failure and the corresponding unchecked receipt in release builds.

### Legacy Catalog ledger admission

- The read-only integrity check admits the variable-sized data before the canonical incident, candidate, activation and terminal journal decoders copy it. Each quarantine record or discard pays for its own body. A historical candidate or activation gets temporary custody, and only the current RepoMap result determines the retained allocation. The allocator, fence, GC floor and invalidation read fixed-length digests without allocating a variable blob. All verification runs in the existing Catalog transaction.

The new regression uses the read-only VFS and a 512 KiB canonical quarantine incident. It verifies insufficient work before record and discard decoding, success with sufficient work, and unchanged Catalog files. The terminal lookup also rejects an oversized journal cell on the owning Catalog test. The pre-edit test request timed out at the shared build lease, before any test ran. Rustfmt and scoped diff checks passed.

### Recovery control settlement

- Q admits one final nonblocking FD3 read after the cancel monitor joins. A queued datagram or receive fault after selection publication leaves a reconciliation result. The durable selection remains available for an exact retry.
- The S resident closes both diagnostic readers on configuration or drain failure and before unwinding into child reap. The existing Host, SDK and Original scopes remain held until reap. A stop observed before spawn retains its typed cause. The authenticated Q terminal and local stop are now separate: when available, the exact terminal goes through the existing journal and archive under the held Original. The response only reports recorded completion after stop if that archive succeeds. A cancel send failure preserves a valid terminal that was already queued by a helper that exited cleanly. A kernel-stalled helper remains an open custody problem.
- A late stop can leave the durable intent if the Original denies journal settlement. The ordinary retry reconstructs FD6 from the exact original FULL and current Source/Host facts. A cold retry with unavailable Source can settle the same operation from the retained intent and the authenticated Q selection witness. See [REQ-03D](#req-03d--ack-유실과-original-종료-정산) for the journal and installed fault results.

The new real publisher case checks the healthy empty queue, queued Cancel, malformed frame and zero-length frame after selection rename and fsync, and observes the exact published bytes for all cases.

The first frozen-input attempt detected a concurrent primary overlay change. The retry captured source and exited 75 while the existing host compile admission was occupied. - The existing Linux queue finished the frozen paired Q x86-64 release build in 23m 12s. The build terminal reports ELF SHA-256 `ce64d9f6ae5698edbb79e6c97d619a0fdd6e2c278163d2a07f152bd51c2da8ec`. The S installed build waits on the existing Root queue while the predecessor SDK container is active. S signing and installed bodies are still downstream. The Q build began before the control settlement patch.

### Reconciliation phase classification

- The Q selection transaction starts journal-backed attempts in reconciliation. Publication can enter before-rename only after matching the canonical bytes and file version of the authenticated original. Unreadable or malformed active control, corrupt retained original and a third active head retain the uncertain outcome without entering the publisher. The completed-terminal check still blocks repeat publication from the original head.
- The retained intent records the first active selection file version in the existing authenticated journal and carries it in FD6 format 4. Q compares it with the locked active file before publishing and rechecks through pre-publication custody. An intact original enters fresh domain validation. Q also writes a recovery publication attribute to the temporary selection before file fsync and rename. It binds the stable request, both canonical selection digests, the first original version and the actual new inode. Reconciliation validates this binding before domain validation and at durability checkpoints. Republishing either head through another operation cannot satisfy the retained request. Earlier records keep their canonical journal encoding. Missing original version or missing candidate attribute yields an uncertain outcome. The deployment filesystem must support file extended attributes.
- The focused Q deployment-selection cycle regression passed 1/1, run `87c020c2-106a-46e9-bc57-a29251c54167`. The pre-edit invocation waited for build admission and compiled the changed source, so there is no observed RED. The public API baseline and test-authority guards passed. The S archive regression request `831682bfe67c32211de167cca69c1269` on `nc-d-candidate-inventory` stopped at shared host compile admission with exit 75, zero bodies. Scoped Rust syntax and 17 S build boundary policies passed.

An independently published candidate and a later Q publication with the same request ID both refuse before the domain callback. - **FAILED before repair:** Q nextest `097e89dd-3e28-40ee-b217-2eed94a82962` executed one body. It failed because the active candidate with an invalid journal SHA was reported as before-rename.
